<script>
      jQuery(window).load(function($){
        setTimeout(function(){
          jQuery(".apple-business-chat-button-container img").attr("src","https://cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/0001/9857/4138/files/apple-image.jpg?262");
        }, 1000);


        function createCookie(name, value, days) {
          var date, expires;
          if (days) {
            date = new Date();
            date.setTime(date.getTime()+(days*24*60*60*1000));
            expires = "; expires="+date.toGMTString();
          } else {
            expires = "";
          }
          document.cookie = name+"="+value+expires+"; path=/";
        }

        window.getCookie = function(name) {
          var match = document.cookie.match(new RegExp('(^| )' + name + '=([^;]+)'));
          if (match) return match[2];
        } 

        // $.cookie("popupnewsletter", null, { path: '/' });

        ck =  getCookie("popupnewsletter");
        // alert(ck);
        if(ck == undefined){
          setTimeout(function(){
            jQuery("#myModal").show();
          }, 3000);

        }
        jQuery("#myModal .closelink").click( function(){
          createCookie("popupnewsletter","Yes",30);
          jQuery("#myModal").hide();
        });

        jQuery("#myModal .close").click( function(){
          createCookie("popupnewsletter","Yes",30);
          jQuery("#myModal").hide();
        });
        jQuery("#myModal .closemobile").click( function(){
          createCookie("popupnewsletter","Yes",30);
          jQuery("#myModal").hide();
        });
        jQuery(".headership").click(function(){
          jQuery("#myModalship").show();
        });

        jQuery("#myModalship .close").click(function(){
          jQuery("#myModalship").hide();	
        });
      }) ;
    </script>

          // This prevents the form from submitting
          event.preventDefault();

          // Capture form input

          // Run the ajax post
          $.ajax({
            url : "https://supergausaservices.com/Responsys/UpdateCustomer",
            type:"POST",
            data: serializedData,
            success: function(data) {
              $(".updateemailmsg").html(data);
              $(".updateemailmsg span").delay(5000).fadeOut('slow');
            }
          });

        /***************** Start Klaviyo Function *************/

        function klaviyoAPI(first_name, last_name, email, source, flag, form) {


          // Klaviyo Newsletter list id
          var list_id = 'Q7GQax';


          if(source == "Homepage popup"){

            var settings = {

              "async": true,

              "crossDomain": true,

              "url": "https://manage.kmail-lists.com/ajax/subscriptions/subscribe",

              "method": "POST",

              "headers": {

                "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded",

                "cache-control": "no-cache"

              },


              "data": {

                "g": list_id,

                "$first_name": first_name,

                "$last_name": last_name,

                "email": email[0],

                "$Gender_type": email[1], 

                "$fields": "$source, $first_name, $last_name,$Gender_type",

                "$source": source

              }

            };


            if(flag == 1){

              settings['data']['$fields'] = "$source, SUPERGA_ACCOUNT, $first_name, $last_name, $Gender_type";

              settings['data']['SUPERGA_ACCOUNT'] = "True";
            }

          }
          else{

            var settings = {

              "async": true,

              "crossDomain": true,

              "url": "https://manage.kmail-lists.com/ajax/subscriptions/subscribe",

              "method": "POST",

              "headers": {

                "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded",

                "cache-control": "no-cache"

              },


              "data": {

                "g": list_id,

                "$first_name": first_name,

                "$last_name": last_name,

                "email": email,

                "$fields": "$source, $first_name, $last_name",

                "$source": source

              }

            };


            if(flag == 1){

              settings['data']['$fields'] = "$source, SUPERGA_ACCOUNT, $first_name, $last_name";

              settings['data']['SUPERGA_ACCOUNT'] = "True";
            }

          } 

          switch (source) {

            case "Homepage popup":

              $.ajax(settings).done(function (response) {

                console.log(response);

                $("#popupnewsletterform").hide();
                $(".popup-title").html("THANKS<br>FOR<br>SIGNING<br>UP!");
                setTimeout(function(){
                  $("#myModal .close").trigger("click");
                }, 3000);

              });

              break;


            case "Site Signups (Footer)":

              $.ajax(settings).done(function (response) {

                console.log(response);

                $(".emsg").html("Your email subscribed successfully.");
                $(".emsg").addClass("sumsg");
                $(".emsg").show();
                $("#footeremail").val("");

              });

              break;



            case "Site Signups (Header)":

              $.ajax(settings).done(function (response) {

                console.log(response);


              });

              break;


            case "Account Creation":

              $.ajax(settings).done(function (response) {

                console.log(response);
                //   $("#create_customer").submit();

              });

              break;


            default: 

              break;
          }

        } 

        /***************** End Klaviyo Function *************/


        /***************** Start Account Creation Klaviyo Function *************/

        $('#createaccountsubmit').click(function(){

          if($('#marketing').prop("checked") == true){

            var form = "create_customer";

            var first_name = jQuery("#FirstName").val();

            var last_name = '';

            last_name = jQuery("#LastName").val();


            var email = jQuery("#Email").val();


            var source = 'Account Creation';

            // flag is for identifing for newsletter or signup
            // 1 is for newsletter
            // 0 for signup

            var flag = 0;

            klaviyoAPI(first_name, last_name, email, source, flag, form);
            console.log(first_name, last_name, email, source, flag, form);          
            // return false;         
          }

          else{               
            //   return false;  
            $("#create_customer").submit();
          }              

        });//end click

        $("#popupsubmitbutton").click(function(){

          // Capture form input  

          var form = "popupnewsletterform";

          // var pname = jQuery("#popupname").val();

          var pemail = $("#popupemail").val();

          var radioValue = '';

          if ($("input[name='gender-type']:checked").size()==0) {

            $(".emsgp").html("Select from Women's / Men's / Both.");
            $(".emsgp").addClass("ermsg");
            $(".emsgp").show();
            return false;
          }
          else {

            radioValue = $("input[name='gender-type']:checked").val();

          }



          if(pemail == "")
          {
            $(".emsgp").html("Enter email address.");
            $(".emsgp").addClass("ermsg");
            $(".emsgp").show();
            return false;
          }
          else
          {
            if(!isValidEmailAddress(pemail)){
              $(".emsgp").html("Email address invalid.");
              $(".emsgp").addClass("ermsg");
              $(".emsgp").show();
              return false;
            }
            else{

              var first_name = '';
              var last_name = '';        
              var email = [pemail,radioValue];
              var source = 'Homepage popup';

              // flag is for identifing for newsletter or signup
              // 1 is for newsletter
              // 0 for signup
              var flag = 1;

              klaviyoAPI(first_name, last_name, email, source, flag, form);

              console.log(first_name, last_name, email, source, flag, form);

              return false;



            }
          }


        });

        function isValidEmailAddress(emailAddress) {
          var pattern = /^([a-z\d!#$%&'*+\-\/=?^_`{|}~\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]+(\.[a-z\d!#$%&'*+\-\/=?^_`{|}~\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]+)*|"((([ \t]*\r\n)?[ \t]+)?([\x01-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f\x7f\x21\x23-\x5b\x5d-\x7e\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]|\\[\x01-\x09\x0b\x0c\x0d-\x7f\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]))*(([ \t]*\r\n)?[ \t]+)?")@(([a-z\d\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]|[a-z\d\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF][a-z\d\-._~\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]*[a-z\d\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF])\.)+([a-z\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]|[a-z\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF][a-z\d\-._~\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]*[a-z\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF])\.?$/i;
          return pattern.test(emailAddress);
        }

        $(".desktopform #emailSignUpSubmitFromHome").click(function(){

          // Capture form input          

          var femail = $(".desktopform #userEmail").val();
          if(femail == "")
          {
            $(".desktopform #homeEmailSignUp").html("Enter email address.");
            $(".desktopform #homeEmailSignUp").addClass("ermsg");
            $(".desktopform #homeEmailSignUp").show();
            return false;
          }
          else
          {
            if(!isValidEmailAddress(femail)){
              $(".desktopform #homeEmailSignUp").html("Email address invalid.");
              $(".desktopform #homeEmailSignUp").addClass("ermsg");
              $(".desktopform #homeEmailSignUp").show();
              return false;
            }
            else{
              $(".desktopform #homeEmailSignUp").show();
              $(".desktopform #homeEmailSignUp").html("<img src='https://cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/0001/9857/4138/t/14/assets/ajax-loader-2.gif'>");  
              var serializedData = $(".desktopform #homeEmailSubscribeForm").serialize();
              serializedData +="&email="+$(".desktopform #userEmail").val();
              console.log(serializedData);
              //debugger;
              // Run the ajax post
              $.ajax({
                url : "https://supergausaservices.com/Responsys/AddNewsLetter",
                type:"POST",
                data: serializedData,
                success: function(data) {
                  if(data.Message == "Success"){
                    $(".desktopform #homeEmailSignUp").hide();
                    jQuery("#myModal2").show();
                    return false;}
                  else{
                    $(".desktopform #homeEmailSignUp").html("Something wrong with email.");
                    $(".desktopform #homeEmailSignUp").addClass("ermsg");
                    $(".desktopform #homeEmailSignUp").show();
                  }
                }
              });
            }
          }


        });

      jQuery(document).ready(function() {
        var url =  window.location.href; 
        jQuery("#redirect_url").val(url);

        jQuery(".ot tr.or").each(function(){
          var aa = String(jQuery(this).children("td.lineitemorder").children("a").html());
          var res = aa.split('/');
          var newstr = res[0]+" / "+res[1];
          jQuery(this).children("td.lineitemorder").children("a").html(newstr);
        });

        jQuery(".ot tr.or").each(function(){
          var aa = String(jQuery(this).children("td.lineitemorder").children("h4").children("a").html());
          var res = aa.split('/');
          var newstr = res[0]+" / "+res[1];
          jQuery(this).children("td.lineitemorder").children("h4").children("a").html(newstr);
        });

        jQuery("#myModal2 .close").click(function(){
          jQuery("#myModal2").hide();
        });
      });   



    </script>
<script>
$(function() {
  // Current Ajax request.
  var currentAjaxRequest = null;
  // Grabbing all search forms on the page, and adding a .search-results list to each.
  var searchForms = $('form[action="/search"]').css('position','relative').each(function() {
    // Grabbing text input.
    var input = $(this).find('input[name="q"]');
    // Adding a list for showing search results.
    var offSet = input.position().top + input.innerHeight();
    $('<ul class="search-results"></ul>').css( { 'position': 'absolute', 'left': '0px', 'top': offSet,'margin-top':'33px' } ).appendTo($(this)).hide();    
    // Listening to keyup and change on the text field within these search forms.
    input.attr('autocomplete', 'off').bind('keyup change', function() {
      // What's the search term?
      var term = $(this).val();
      // What's the search form?
      var form = $(this).closest('form');
      // What's the search URL?
      var searchURL = '/search?type=product&q=' + term;
      // What's the search results list?
      var resultsList = form.find('.search-results');
      // If that's a new term and it contains at least 3 characters.
      if (term.length > 3 && term != $(this).attr('data-old-term')) {
        // Saving old query.
        $(this).attr('data-old-term', term);
        // Killing any Ajax request that's currently being processed.
        if (currentAjaxRequest != null) currentAjaxRequest.abort();
        // Pulling results.
        currentAjaxRequest = $.getJSON(searchURL + '&view=json', function(data) {
          // Reset results.
          resultsList.empty();
          // If we have no results.
          if(data.results_count == 0) {
            // resultsList.html('<li><span class="title">No results.</span></li>');
            // resultsList.fadeIn(200);
            resultsList.hide();
          } else {
            // If we have results.
            $.each(data.results, function(index, item) {
              var link = $('<a class="flex flex-align-start flex-nowrap flex-justify-beetween"></a>').attr('href', item.url);
              if(item.compare != item.price)
              {link.append('<span class="title">' + item.title + '<br><strike>'+item.compare+'</strike> '+item.price+'</span>');}
              else
              {link.append('<span class="title">' + item.title + '<br>'+item.price+'</span>');}
              link.append('<span class="thumbnail"><img src="' + item.thumbnail + '" /></span>');
              link.wrap('<li></li>');
              resultsList.append(link.parent());
            });
            // The Ajax request will return at the most 10 results.
            // If there are more than 10, let's link to the search results page.
            if(data.results_count > 10) {
              resultsList.append('<li><span class="title"><a href="' + searchURL + '">See all results (' + data.results_count + ')</a></span></li>');
            }
            resultsList.after('<a href="javascript:;" class="close" title="Close"></a>');
            resultsList.fadeIn(200).addClass('op');
          }        
        });
      }
    });
  });
  // Clicking outside makes the results disappear.
  $('body').bind('click', function(){
    $('.search-results').hide().removeClass('op');
  });
});
</script>

<script>

/**
 * Module to ajaxify all add to cart forms on the page.
 *
 * Copyright (c) 2015 Caroline Schnapp (11heavens.com)
 * Dual licensed under the MIT and GPL licenses:
 * http://www.opensource.org/licenses/mit-license.php
 * http://www.gnu.org/licenses/gpl.html
 *
 */
Shopify.AjaxifyCart = (function($) {
  
  // Some configuration options.
  // I have separated what you will never need to change from what
  // you might change.
  
  var _config = {
    
    // What you might want to change
    addToCartBtnLabel:             'Add to Bag',
    addedToCartBtnLabel:           'Thank you!',
    addingToCartBtnLabel:          'Adding...',
    soldOutBtnLabel:               'Sold Out',
    howLongTillBtnReturnsToNormal: 1000, // in milliseconds.
    cartCountSelector:             '.cart_count, .cart-count, #cart-count a:first, #gocart p a, #cart .checkout em, .item-count',
    cartTotalSelector:             '#cart-price',
    // 'aboveForm' for top of add to cart form, 
    // 'belowForm' for below the add to cart form, and 
    // 'nextButton' for next to add to cart button.
    feedbackPosition:              'belowForm',
    
    // What you will never need to change
    addToCartBtnSelector:          '[type="submit"]',
    addToCartBtnSelectordiv:          '.product-form__item product-form__item--submit',
    addToCartFormSelector:         'form[action="/cart/add"]',
    shopifyAjaxAddURL:             '/cart/add.js',
    shopifyAjaxCartURL:            '/cart.js'
  };
  
  // We need some feedback when adding an item to the cart.
  // Here it is.  
  var _showFeedback = function(success, html, $addToCartForm) {
    $('.ajaxified-cart-feedback').remove();
    var feedback = '<br/><p class="ajaxified-cart-feedback ' + success + '">' + html + '</p>';
    var $addToCartBtndiv = $addToCartForm.find(".product-form__item--submit");
    switch (_config.feedbackPosition) {
      case 'aboveForm':
        $addToCartForm.before(feedback);
        break;
      case 'belowForm':
        $addToCartBtndiv.after(feedback);
        break;
      case 'nextButton':
      default:
        $addToCartForm.find(_config.addToCartBtnSelector).after(feedback);
        break;   
    }
    // If you use animate.css
    // $('.ajaxified-cart-feedback').addClass('animated bounceInDown');
    $('.ajaxified-cart-feedback').slideDown();
  };
  var _setText = function($button, label) {
    if ($button.children().length) {
      $button.children().each(function() {
        if ($.trim($(this).text()) !== '') {
          $(this).text(label);
        }
      });
    }
    else {
      $button.val(label).text(label);
    }
  };
  var _init = function() {   
    $(document).ready(function() { 
      $(_config.addToCartFormSelector).submit(function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        var $addToCartForm = $(this);
        var $addToCartBtn = $addToCartForm.find(_config.addToCartBtnSelector);
        _setText($addToCartBtn, _config.addingToCartBtnLabel);
        $addToCartBtn.addClass('disabled').prop('disabled', true);
        // Add to cart.
        $.ajax({
          url: _config.shopifyAjaxAddURL,
          dataType: 'json',
          type: 'post',
          data: $addToCartForm.serialize(),
          success: function(itemData) {
            // Re-enable add to cart button.
            $addToCartBtn.addClass('inverted');
            //_setText($addToCartBtn, _config.addedToCartBtnLabel);
            _showFeedback('success','<i class="fa fa-check"></i> Added to cart! <a href="/cart">View cart</a> or <a href="/collections/all">continue shopping</a>.',$addToCartForm);
            window.setTimeout(function(){
              $addToCartBtn.prop('disabled', false).removeClass('disabled').removeClass('inverted');
              _setText($addToCartBtn,_config.addToCartBtnLabel);
            }, _config.howLongTillBtnReturnsToNormal);
            // Update cart count and show cart link.
            //$("#crt").load(location.href + " #mini-shopping-cart");
            $.get( "/cart?view=mini", function( data ) {
              $('.mini-shopping-cart').html(data);
              $('#mini-shopping-cart').removeClass('loading');
            });
            $(".cartDrawer").toggleClass("active");
            $(".overlay").fadeIn('slow');
            $("#mini-shopping-cart").fadeIn('slow');
            
            $.getJSON(_config.shopifyAjaxCartURL, function(cart) {
              if (_config.cartCountSelector && $(_config.cartCountSelector).size()) {
                var value = $(_config.cartCountSelector).html() || '0';
                $(_config.cartCountSelector).html(value.replace(/[0-9]+/,cart.item_count)).removeClass('hidden-count');
              }
              if (_config.cartTotalSelector && $(_config.cartTotalSelector).size()) {
                if (typeof Currency !== 'undefined' && typeof Currency.moneyFormats !== 'undefined') {
                  var newCurrency = '';
                  if ($('[name="currencies"]').size()) {
                    newCurrency = $('[name="currencies"]').val();
                  }
                  else if ($('#currencies span.selected').size()) {
                    newCurrency = $('#currencies span.selected').attr('data-currency');
                  }
                  if (newCurrency) {
                    $(_config.cartTotalSelector).html('<span class=money>' + Shopify.formatMoney(Currency.convert(cart.total_price, "USD", newCurrency), Currency.money_format[newCurrency]) + '</span>');
                  } 
                  else {
                    $(_config.cartTotalSelector).html(Shopify.formatMoney(cart.total_price, "${{amount}}"));
                  }
                }
                else {
                  $(_config.cartTotalSelector).html(Shopify.formatMoney(cart.total_price, "${{amount}}"));
                }
              };
            });        
          }, 
          error: function(XMLHttpRequest) {
            var response = eval('(' + XMLHttpRequest.responseText + ')');
            response = response.description;
            if (response.slice(0,4) === 'All ') {
              _showFeedback('error', response.replace('All 1 ', 'All '), $addToCartForm);
              $addToCartBtn.prop('disabled', false);
              _setText($addToCartBtn, _config.soldOutBtnLabel);
              $addToCartBtn.prop('disabled',true);
            }
            else {
              _showFeedback('error', '<i class="fa fa-warning"></i> ' + response, $addToCartForm);
              $addToCartBtn.prop('disabled', false).removeClass('disabled');
              _setText($addToCartBtn, _config.addToCartBtnLabel);
            }
          }
        });   
        return false;    
      });
    });
  };
  return {
    init: function(params) {
        // Configuration
        params = params || {};
        // Merging with defaults.
        $.extend(_config, params);
        // Action
        $(function() {
          _init();
        });
    },    
    getConfig: function() {
      return _config;
    }
  }  
})(jQuery);

Shopify.AjaxifyCart.init();

<script>
 ShowMessage();
var myVar ;
if(window.location.href.indexOf('/products')>-1)
{
  myVar=setInterval(checkpreorder, 1000);
}
    
  
  function checkpreorder() {
    if($("#pre-order").is(':visible'))
    {
      $("#pre-order").click(function()                          
      {        
      	 AddCookie(meta.product.id,$(".tooltipster-content").html(),365);   
       	setTimeout(function(){ ShowMessage() }, 2500);
      });
      
      clearInterval(myVar);
    }
}
	function AddCookie(cname, cvalue, exdays) 
    {
    	var d = new Date();
    	d.setTime(d.getTime() + (exdays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
    	var expires = "expires="+d.toUTCString();
    	document.cookie = cname + "=" + cvalue + ";" + expires + ";path=/";
	}

	function getCookie(cname) 
    {
    	var name = cname + "=";
    	var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
    	for(var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) 
        {
        	var c = ca[i];
        	while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') 
            {
            	c = c.substring(1);
        	}
        	if (c.indexOf(name) == 0) 
            {
            	return c.substring(name.length, c.length);
        	}
    	}
    	return "";
	}


  function ShowMessage()
  {
            
    if($("[data-product-id]").length>0)
    {          
      $("[data-product-id]").each(function()
      {        
         var preorderMessage = getCookie($(this).attr("data-product-id"));      
         var propertylength = $(this).parents('td').find('.cart__meta-text:contains(Pre-ordered)').length;          
         var checkoutproperty = $(this).find('.product__description__property:contains(Pre-ordered)').length;                             
         if(preorderMessage != undefined && preorderMessage!=null && preorderMessage!="" && propertylength>0)
         {           
            $(this).html(preorderMessage);
         }
         if(preorderMessage != undefined && preorderMessage!=null && location.href.indexOf('/checkouts')>-1 && checkoutproperty >0)
         {
           	if($(this).find('.custom_message').length==0)
            {
              	$(this).find('.product__description').append('<span class="custom_message" style="color:red">'+preorderMessage +' </span>');
            }           
         }
         
      });
    }
  }
  
  
  function getPre_Items()
  {
          var pre_Items="";               
          $(".product-table tbody tr").each(function()
          {                    

            if($(this).find('.product__description__property:contains("Pre-ordered")').length>0)
            {
              var pId = $(this).attr('data-product-id');
              var message=getCookie(pId);                        
              if(message != null && message.trim()!='')
              {
                if(pre_Items=="")
                {
                  pre_Items +="Pre_"+$(this).attr('data-variant-id') +"#"+ message;
                }
                else 
                {
                  pre_Items +="~Pre_"+$(this).attr('data-variant-id') +"#"+ message;
                }
              }                     
              // remove from cookie
             // AddCookie(pId,null,-10);
            }
          });
          return pre_Items;
  }

   }
